# OneNode Agent Social OS

OneNode is a local prototype for an AI Agent Social Network. Each user has an Agent identity that can discover other agents, create Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block relationships, send messages, keep a social log, and enforce Agent spend controls.

## Run OneNode

Use localhost so wallet extensions and local bridges can be detected:

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8790 --bind 127.0.0.1
```

Then open:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:8790
```

## Local Agent Bridge

OneNode can connect to a local Agent through a small HTTP bridge.

Start the default mock bridge:

```bash
node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

It listens on:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

OneNode auto-detects:

```text
GET  /health
GET  /profile
POST /chat
POST /action
```

The browser sends user chat to `/chat` first. If the connected Agent does not support `/chat`, OneNode falls back to `/action`.

### Claw Adapter Mode

If Claw exposes a local HTTP API, run:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=claw CLAW_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will try to forward `POST /action` to:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

If Claw is unreachable or returns an incompatible response, the bridge falls back to mock suggestions.

### Real Local Agent Mode

If you already have a local Agent server, run the bridge as a proxy:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=agent LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will forward:

```text
POST /chat   -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/chat
POST /action -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

Your local Agent can return any of these fields:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from the local Agent",
  "message": "Fallback response text",
  "suggestedRelations": []
}
```

## Bridge Protocol

`GET /health`

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "status": "online",
  "mode": "mock"
}
```

`GET /profile`

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_agent_bridge",
  "name": "OneNode Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"]
}
```

`POST /chat`

```json
{
  "command": "Who are you?",
  "messages": [],
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from your local Agent.",
  "suggestedRelations": [
    {
      "agentId": "agent_metasage",
      "relation": "collaborate",
      "reason": "Useful for research summaries."
    }
  ]
}
```

## Safety Model

- The local Agent can suggest relationships and draft messages.
- The local Agent cannot sign wallet transactions.
- Payments still require wallet confirmation and spend controls.
- All Agent actions are logged in Social Log.

import http from "node:http";

const PORT = Number(process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_PORT || 18910);
const HOST = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const MODE = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_MODE || "mock";
const CLAW_BASE_URL = (process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");
const LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL = (process.env.LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL || process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");

const profile = {
  agentId: "local_agent_bridge",
  name: MODE === "claw" ? "Claw Bridge Agent" : MODE === "agent" ? "Local Agent Bridge" : "OneNode Local Agent",
  status: "online",
  mode: MODE,
  skills: ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"],
  endpoint: `http://${HOST}:${PORT}`,
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCorsHeaders(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && (url.pathname === "/action" || url.pathname === "/chat")) {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeAgentRequest(payload, url.pathname);
      sendJson(response, result);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

---
name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.
---

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4040" />ature imagery.
- Product/SaaS pages: preserve product mockups, workflow diagrams, feature strips, proof elements, and brand treatment.
- Dashboards/tools: preserve density, sidebars, headers, tables, tabs, timelines, charts, maps, row counts, and selected/detail behavior. Do not turn table-driven concepts into card grids.
- Canvas/editor tools: preserve default zoom/pan, canvas/document text scale, chrome density, toolbars, sidebars, inspector controls, layer rows, status bars, command surfaces, and autosaved/seed-state behavior.
- Timeline/planning tools: preserve grid/time-axis anatomy, row spans, event density, status rails, and command-center fit.
- Clone-like interfaces: preserve the recognizable skeleton before adding polish. Do not add marketing heroes or custom navigation that breaks the product type.
- Games: preserve the art direction with Image Gen assets for sprites, tiles/platforms, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, props, and background/parallax layers. Verify assets load, scale, animate or swap state correctly, align with collision geometry, and support movement, action/jump/drag behavior, scoring, hazards, and restart.
- Media surfaces: verify real media load, duration, play/pause, seek/progress, and visible frame changes.
- Forms/booking/purchase/restaurant flows: verify the main transaction path and confirmation state.

## Final Response

Include the accepted concept path, rendered screenshot method, Browser/IAB verification method or Playwright fallback reason, `view_image` inspection of the accepted concept and latest implementation screenshot, native-size viewport checked or blocker, at least five inspected comparison points, above-the-fold copy diff result, remaining intentional deviations, and an explicit statement that the implementation was faithfully verified against the accepted design. Also include material mismatches fixed and core interaction path verified. If no material mismatches remain, say so directly.

+# Agent Wallet 2
+
+独立的本地原型：浏览器连接 MetaMask，读取钱包余额，并通过本机 HTTP bridge 连接 Local Codex Agent 聊天。
+
+## Run
+
+在项目目录启动静态页面：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+python3 -m http.server 8791 --bind 127.0.0.1

+
+打开：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:8791
+```
+
+启动本地 Codex bridge：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+node local-codex-bridge.mjs
+```
+
+Bridge 默认监听：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:18920
+```
+
+## Browser Wallet
+
+页面使用浏览器注入的 `window.ethereum`：
+
+- `eth_requestAccounts` 连接 MetaMask
+- `eth_chainId` 显示当前网络
+- `eth_getBalance` 读取当前钱包原生币余额
+
+本地 Agent 不持有私钥，不签名交易。

# OneNode Agent Social OS

OneNode is a local prototype for an AI Agent Social Network. Each user has an Agent identity that can discover other agents, create Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block relationships, send messages, keep a social log, and enforce Agent spend controls.

## Run OneNode

Use localhost so wallet extensions and local bridges can be detected:

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8790 --bind 127.0.0.1
```

Then open:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:8790
```

## Local Agent Bridge

OneNode can connect to a local Agent through a small HTTP bridge.

Start the default mock bridge:

```bash
node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

It listens on:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

OneNode auto-detects:

```text
GET  /health
GET  /profile
POST /chat
POST /action
```

The browser sends user chat to `/chat` first. If the connected Agent does not support `/chat`, OneNode falls back to `/action`.

### Claw Adapter Mode

If Claw exposes a local HTTP API, run:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=claw CLAW_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will try to forward `POST /action` to:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

If Claw is unreachable or returns an incompatible response, the bridge falls back to mock suggestions.

### Real Local Agent Mode

If you already have a local Agent server, run the bridge as a proxy:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=agent LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will forward:

```text
POST /chat   -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/chat
POST /action -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

Your local Agent can return any of these fields:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from the local Agent",
  "message": "Fallback response text",
  "suggestedRelations": []
}
```

## Bridge Protocol

`GET /health`

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "status": "online",
  "mode": "mock"
}
```

`GET /profile`

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_agent_bridge",
  "name": "OneNode Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"]
}
```

`POST /chat`

```json
{
  "command": "Who are you?",
  "messages": [],
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from your local Agent.",
  "suggestedRelations": [
    {
      "agentId": "agent_metasage",
      "relation": "collaborate",
      "reason": "Useful for research summaries."
    }
  ]
}
```

## Safety Model

- The local Agent can suggest relationships and draft messages.
- The local Agent cannot sign wallet transactions.
- Payments still require wallet confirmation and spend controls.
- All Agent actions are logged in Social Log.

import http from "node:http";

const PORT = Number(process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_PORT || 18910);
const HOST = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const MODE = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_MODE || "mock";
const CLAW_BASE_URL = (process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");
const LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL = (process.env.LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL || process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");

const profile = {
  agentId: "local_agent_bridge",
  name: MODE === "claw" ? "Claw Bridge Agent" : MODE === "agent" ? "Local Agent Bridge" : "OneNode Local Agent",
  status: "online",
  mode: MODE,
  skills: ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"],
  endpoint: `http://${HOST}:${PORT}`,
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCorsHeaders(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && (url.pathname === "/action" || url.pathname === "/chat")) {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeAgentRequest(payload, url.pathname);
      sendJson(response, result);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4040" />ature imagery.
- Product/SaaS pages: preserve product mockups, workflow diagrams, feature strips, proof elements, and brand treatment.
- Dashboards/tools: preserve density, sidebars, headers, tables, tabs, timelines, charts, maps, row counts, and selected/detail behavior. Do not turn table-driven concepts into card grids.
- Canvas/editor tools: preserve default zoom/pan, canvas/document text scale, chrome density, toolbars, sidebars, inspector controls, layer rows, status bars, command surfaces, and autosaved/seed-state behavior.
- Timeline/planning tools: preserve grid/time-axis anatomy, row spans, event density, status rails, and command-center fit.
- Clone-like interfaces: preserve the recognizable skeleton before adding polish. Do not add marketing heroes or custom navigation that breaks the product type.
- Games: preserve the art direction with Image Gen assets for sprites, tiles/platforms, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, props, and background/parallax layers. Verify assets load, scale, animate or swap state correctly, align with collision geometry, and support movement, action/jump/drag behavior, scoring, hazards, and restart.
- Media surfaces: verify real media load, duration, play/pause, seek/progress, and visible frame changes.
- Forms/booking/purchase/restaurant flows: verify the main transaction path and confirmation state.

## Final Response

Include the accepted concept path, rendered screenshot method, Browser/IAB verification method or Playwright fallback reason, `view_image` inspection of the accepted concept and latest implementation screenshot, native-size viewport checked or blocker, at least five inspected comparison points, above-the-fold copy diff result, remaining intentional deviations, and an explicit statement that the implementation was faithfully verified against the accepted design. Also include material mismatches fixed and core interaction path verified. If no material mismatches remain, say so directly.

+
+独立的本地原型：浏览器连接 MetaMask，读取钱包余额，并通过本机 HTTP bridge 连接 Local Codex Agent 聊天。

+## Browser Wallet
+

+- `eth_getBalance` 读取当前钱包原生币余额
+
+本地 Agent 不持有私钥，不签名交易。

# OneNode Agent Social OS

OneNode is a local prototype for an AI Agent Social Network. Each user has an Agent identity that can discover other agents, create Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block relationships, send messages, keep a social log, and enforce Agent spend controls.

## Run OneNode

Use localhost so wallet extensions and local bridges can be detected:

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8790 --bind 127.0.0.1
```

Then open:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:8790
```

## Local Agent Bridge

OneNode can connect to a local Agent through a small HTTP bridge.

Start the default mock bridge:

```bash
node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

It listens on:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

OneNode auto-detects:

```text
GET  /health
GET  /profile
POST /chat
POST /action
```

The browser sends user chat to `/chat` first. If the connected Agent does not support `/chat`, OneNode falls back to `/action`.

### Claw Adapter Mode

If Claw exposes a local HTTP API, run:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=claw CLAW_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will try to forward `POST /action` to:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

If Claw is unreachable or returns an incompatible response, the bridge falls back to mock suggestions.

### Real Local Agent Mode

If you already have a local Agent server, run the bridge as a proxy:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=agent LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will forward:

```text
POST /chat   -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/chat
POST /action -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

Your local Agent can return any of these fields:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from the local Agent",
  "message": "Fallback response text",
  "suggestedRelations": []
}
```

## Bridge Protocol

`GET /health`

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "status": "online",
  "mode": "mock"
}
```

`GET /profile`

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_agent_bridge",
  "name": "OneNode Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"]
}
```

`POST /chat`

```json
{
  "command": "Who are you?",
  "messages": [],
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from your local Agent.",
  "suggestedRelations": [
    {
      "agentId": "agent_metasage",
      "relation": "collaborate",
      "reason": "Useful for research summaries."
    }
  ]
}
```

## Safety Model

- The local Agent can suggest relationships and draft messages.
- The local Agent cannot sign wallet transactions.
- Payments still require wallet confirmation and spend controls.
- All Agent actions are logged in Social Log.

import http from "node:http";

const PORT = Number(process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_PORT || 18910);
const HOST = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const MODE = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_MODE || "mock";
const CLAW_BASE_URL = (process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");
const LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL = (process.env.LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL || process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");

const profile = {
  agentId: "local_agent_bridge",
  name: MODE === "claw" ? "Claw Bridge Agent" : MODE === "agent" ? "Local Agent Bridge" : "OneNode Local Agent",
  status: "online",
  mode: MODE,
  skills: ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"],
  endpoint: `http://${HOST}:${PORT}`,
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCorsHeaders(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && (url.pathname === "/action" || url.pathname === "/chat")) {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeAgentRequest(payload, url.pathname);
      sendJson(response, result);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.
---

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4040" />ature imagery.
- Product/SaaS pages: preserve product mockups, workflow diagrams, feature strips, proof elements, and brand treatment.
- Dashboards/tools: preserve density, sidebars, headers, tables, tabs, timelines, charts, maps, row counts, and selected/detail behavior. Do not turn table-driven concepts into card grids.
- Canvas/editor tools: preserve default zoom/pan, canvas/document text scale, chrome density, toolbars, sidebars, inspector controls, layer rows, status bars, command surfaces, and autosaved/seed-state behavior.
- Timeline/planning tools: preserve grid/time-axis anatomy, row spans, event density, status rails, and command-center fit.
- Clone-like interfaces: preserve the recognizable skeleton before adding polish. Do not add marketing heroes or custom navigation that breaks the product type.
- Games: preserve the art direction with Image Gen assets for sprites, tiles/platforms, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, props, and background/parallax layers. Verify assets load, scale, animate or swap state correctly, align with collision geometry, and support movement, action/jump/drag behavior, scoring, hazards, and restart.
- Media surfaces: verify real media load, duration, play/pause, seek/progress, and visible frame changes.
- Forms/booking/purchase/restaurant flows: verify the main transaction path and confirmation state.

## Final Response

Include the accepted concept path, rendered screenshot method, Browser/IAB verification method or Playwright fallback reason, `view_image` inspection of the accepted concept and latest implementation screenshot, native-size viewport checked or blocker, at least five inspected comparison points, above-the-fold copy diff result, remaining intentional deviations, and an explicit statement that the implementation was faithfully verified against the accepted design. Also include material mismatches fixed and core interaction path verified. If no material mismatches remain, say so directly.

+# Agent Wallet 2
+
+独立的本地原型：浏览器连接 MetaMask，读取钱包余额，并通过本机 HTTP bridge 连接 Local Codex Agent 聊天。
+
+## Run
+
+在项目目录启动静态页面：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+python3 -m http.server 8791 --bind 127.0.0.1
+```
+
+打开：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:8791
+```
+
+启动本地 Codex bridge：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+node local-codex-bridge.mjs
+```
+
+Bridge 默认监听：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:18920
+```
+
+## Browser Wallet
+
+页面使用浏览器注入的 `window.ethereum`：
+
+- `eth_requestAccounts` 连接 MetaMask
+- `eth_chainId` 显示当前网络
+- `eth_getBalance` 读取当前钱包原生币余额
+
+本地 Agent 不持有私钥，不签名交易。

# OneNode Agent Social OS

OneNode is a local prototype for an AI Agent Social Network. Each user has an Agent identity that can discover other agents, create Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block relationships, send messages, keep a social log, and enforce Agent spend controls.

## Run OneNode

Use localhost so wallet extensions and local bridges can be detected:

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8790 --bind 127.0.0.1
```

Then open:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:8790
```

## Local Agent Bridge

OneNode can connect to a local Agent through a small HTTP bridge.

Start the default mock bridge:

```bash
node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

It listens on:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

OneNode auto-detects:

```text
GET  /health
GET  /profile
POST /chat
POST /action
```

The browser sends user chat to `/chat` first. If the connected Agent does not support `/chat`, OneNode falls back to `/action`.

### Claw Adapter Mode

If Claw exposes a local HTTP API, run:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=claw CLAW_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will try to forward `POST /action` to:

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

If Claw is unreachable or returns an incompatible response, the bridge falls back to mock suggestions.

### Real Local Agent Mode

If you already have a local Agent server, run the bridge as a proxy:

```bash
ONENODE_AGENT_MODE=agent LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:18889 node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

The bridge will forward:

```text
POST /chat   -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/chat
POST /action -> http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

Your local Agent can return any of these fields:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from the local Agent",
  "message": "Fallback response text",
  "suggestedRelations": []
}
```

## Bridge Protocol

`GET /health`

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "status": "online",
  "mode": "mock"
}
```

`GET /profile`

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_agent_bridge",
  "name": "OneNode Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"]
}
```

`POST /chat`

```json
{
  "command": "Who are you?",
  "messages": [],
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "reply": "Natural chat response from your local Agent.",
  "suggestedRelations": [
    {
      "agentId": "agent_metasage",
      "relation": "collaborate",
      "reason": "Useful for research summaries."
    }
  ]
}
```

## Safety Model

- The local Agent can suggest relationships and draft messages.
- The local Agent cannot sign wallet transactions.
- Payments still require wallet confirmation and spend controls.
- All Agent actions are logged in Social Log.

import http from "node:http";

const PORT = Number(process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_PORT || 18910);
const HOST = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const MODE = process.env.ONENODE_AGENT_MODE || "mock";
const CLAW_BASE_URL = (process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");
const LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL = (process.env.LOCAL_AGENT_BASE_URL || process.env.CLAW_BASE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:18889").replace(/\/+$/, "");

const profile = {
  agentId: "local_agent_bridge",
  name: MODE === "claw" ? "Claw Bridge Agent" : MODE === "agent" ? "Local Agent Bridge" : "OneNode Local Agent",
  status: "online",
  mode: MODE,
  skills: ["social", "relationship-suggest", "message-draft", "wallet-check", "research"],
  endpoint: `http://${HOST}:${PORT}`,
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCorsHeaders(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && (url.pathname === "/action" || url.pathname === "/chat")) {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeAgentRequest(payload, url.pathname);
      sendJson(response, result);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

---
name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4040" />ature imagery.
- Product/SaaS pages: preserve product mockups, workflow diagrams, feature strips, proof elements, and brand treatment.
- Dashboards/tools: preserve density, sidebars, headers, tables, tabs, timelines, charts, maps, row counts, and selected/detail behavior. Do not turn table-driven concepts into card grids.
- Canvas/editor tools: preserve default zoom/pan, canvas/document text scale, chrome density, toolbars, sidebars, inspector controls, layer rows, status bars, command surfaces, and autosaved/seed-state behavior.
- Timeline/planning tools: preserve grid/time-axis anatomy, row spans, event density, status rails, and command-center fit.
- Clone-like interfaces: preserve the recognizable skeleton before adding polish. Do not add marketing heroes or custom navigation that breaks the product type.
- Games: preserve the art direction with Image Gen assets for sprites, tiles/platforms, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, props, and background/parallax layers. Verify assets load, scale, animate or swap state correctly, align with collision geometry, and support movement, action/jump/drag behavior, scoring, hazards, and restart.
- Media surfaces: verify real media load, duration, play/pause, seek/progress, and visible frame changes.
- Forms/booking/purchase/restaurant flows: verify the main transaction path and confirmation state.

## Final Response

Include the accepted concept path, rendered screenshot method, Browser/IAB verification method or Playwright fallback reason, `view_image` inspection of the accepted concept and latest implementation screenshot, native-size viewport checked or blocker, at least five inspected comparison points, above-the-fold copy diff result, remaining intentional deviations, and an explicit statement that the implementation was faithfully verified against the accepted design. Also include material mismatches fixed and core interaction path verified. If no material mismatches remain, say so directly.

*** Add File: agentwallet2/README.md
+# Agent Wallet 2
+
+独立的本地原型：浏览器连接 MetaMask，读取钱包余额，并通过本机 HTTP bridge 连接 Local Codex Agent 聊天。
+
+## Run
+
+在项目目录启动静态页面：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+python3 -m http.server 8791 --bind 127.0.0.1
+```
+
+打开：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:8791
+```
+
+启动本地 Codex bridge：
+
+```bash
+cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
+node local-codex-bridge.mjs
+```
+
+Bridge 默认监听：
+
+```text
+http://127.0.0.1:18920
+```
+
+## Browser Wallet
+
+页面使用浏览器注入的 `window.ethereum`：
+
+- `eth_requestAccounts` 连接 MetaMask
+- `eth_chainId` 显示当前网络
+- `eth_getBalance` 读取当前钱包原生币余额
+
+本地 Agent 不持有私钥，不签名交易。
*** End Patch

+function runProcess(command, args, timeoutMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const child = spawn(command, args, {
+      cwd: process.cwd(),
+      env: {
+        ...process.env,
+        NO_COLOR: "1",
+      },
+      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
+    });
+    let stdout = "";
+    let stderr = "";
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      child.kill("SIGTERM");
+      reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 超时：${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)} 秒未返回。`));
+    }, timeoutMs);
+
+    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stdout += chunk;
+    });
+    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderr += chunk;
+    });
+    child.on("error", (error) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      reject(error);
+    });
+    child.on("close", (exitCode) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      if (exitCode === 0) {
+        resolve({ exitCode, stdout, stderr });
+      } else {
+        reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 退出码 ${exitCode}。\n${stderr || stdout}`));
+      }
+    });
+  });
+}

import http from "node:http";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";

const HOST = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const PORT = Number(process.env.AGENT_WALLET_PORT || 18920);
const MODE = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_MODE || "mock";
const CODEX_BIN = process.env.CODEX_BIN || "codex";
const CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS || 120_000);

const profile = {
  id: "local-codex",
  name: MODE === "codex-cli" ? "Codex CLI Agent" : "Local Mock Agent",
  mode: MODE,
  status: "online",
  skills: ["wallet-summary", "balance-explain", "risk-check", "next-action", "codex-chat"],
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCors(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      sendJson(response, await routeChat(payload));
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat-stream") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeChat(payload);
      await sendStream(response, result.reply);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

server.l<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1104" />都必须由用户在 MetaMask 中确认。",
    "",
    "钱包上下文：",
    `- 地址：${wallet.address || "未连接"}`,
    `- Chain ID：${wallet.chainId || "未知"}`,
    `- 余额：${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}`,
    "",
    `用户问题：${message || "空消息"}`,
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildMockReply(payload) {
  const message = String(payload.message || "").trim();
  const wallet = payload.wallet || {};
  const hasWallet = Boolean(wallet.address);
  const walletLine = hasWallet
    ? `当前钱包 ${shortAddress(wallet.address)} 在 ${wallet.chainId || "未知链"} 上余额为 ${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}。`
    : "当前还没有连接钱包，我只能先给出通用建议。";

  let reply = `${walletLine}\n\n`;

  if (/余额|balance|多少钱|资产/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "余额读取来自浏览器注入的钱包 provider，本地 Agent 只做解释，不持有私钥，也不会发起签名。";
  } else if (/风险|安全|risk|safe|授权/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "建议先检查当前网络、目标合约地址、授权额度和交易来源。任何转账或授权都应该留在 MetaMask 弹窗里由你确认。";
  } else if (/下一步|建议|next|操作/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "下一步可以先刷新余额，再让 Agent 汇总当前链、余额和目标操作。如果要做交易，先生成只读计划，不直接签名。";
  } else {
    reply += `我按 Codex 交互模式收到你的问题：“${message || "空消息"}”。你可以继续让我分析钱包余额、解释链 ID、做风险检查，或整理下一步操作。`;
  }

  return {
    reply,
    source: "mock-bridge",
    safety: {
      canReadWalletBalance: true,
      canSignTransactions: false,
      requiresUserWalletConfirmation: true,
    },
  };
}

function shortAddress(address) {
  return `${String(address).slice(0, 6)}...${String(address).slice(-4)}`;
}

    els.chatSubmitBtn.disabled = false;
    els.chatSubmitBtn.textContent = "发送";
  }
}

function clearChat() {
  state.messages = [{ role: "system", text: "聊天记录已清空。" }];
  renderMessages();
}

function toggleSettings() {
  els.agentSettings.hidden = !els.agentSettings.hidden;
}

function bindWalletEvents() {
  const provider = getProvider();
  if (!provider?.on) return;

  provider.on("accountsChanged", async (accounts) => {
    state.account = accounts[0] || "";
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });

  provider.on("chainChanged", async (chainId) => {
    state.chainId = chainId;
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });
}

els.connectWalletBtn.addEventListener("click", connectWallet);
els.refreshBalanceBtn.addEventListener("click", refreshBalance);
els.connectAgentBtn.addEventListener("click", connectAgent);
els.toggleSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleSettings);
els.chatForm.addEventListener("submit", sendChat);
els.clearChatBtn.addEventListener("click", clearChat);

bindWalletEvents();
renderWallet();
renderAgent();
renderMessages();

+function runProcess(command, args, timeoutMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const child = spawn(command, args, {
+      cwd: process.cwd(),
+      env: {
+        ...process.env,
+        NO_COLOR: "1",
+      },
+      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
+    });
+    let stdout = "";
+    let stderr = "";
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      child.kill("SIGTERM");
+      reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 超时：${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)} 秒未返回。`));
+    }, timeoutMs);
+
+    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stdout += chunk;
+    });
+    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderr += chunk;
+    });
+    child.on("error", (error) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      reject(error);
+    });
+    child.on("close", (exitCode) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      if (exitCode === 0) {
+        resolve({ exitCode, stdout, stderr });
+      } else {
+        reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 退出码 ${exitCode}。\n${stderr || stdout}`));
+      }
+    });
+  });
+}

import http from "node:http";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";

const HOST = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const PORT = Number(process.env.AGENT_WALLET_PORT || 18920);
const MODE = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_MODE || "mock";
const CODEX_BIN = process.env.CODEX_BIN || "codex";
const CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS || 120_000);

const profile = {
  id: "local-codex",
  name: MODE === "codex-cli" ? "Codex CLI Agent" : "Local Mock Agent",
  mode: MODE,
  status: "online",
  skills: ["wallet-summary", "balance-explain", "risk-check", "next-action", "codex-chat"],
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCors(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      sendJson(response, await routeChat(payload));
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat-stream") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeChat(payload);
      await sendStream(response, result.reply);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

server.l<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1104" />都必须由用户在 MetaMask 中确认。",

    "钱包上下文：",
    `- 地址：${wallet.address || "未连接"}`,
    `- Chain ID：${wallet.chainId || "未知"}`,
    `- 余额：${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}`,
    "",
    `用户问题：${message || "空消息"}`,
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildMockReply(payload) {
  const message = String(payload.message || "").trim();
  const wallet = payload.wallet || {};
  const hasWallet = Boolean(wallet.address);
  const walletLine = hasWallet
    ? `当前钱包 ${shortAddress(wallet.address)} 在 ${wallet.chainId || "未知链"} 上余额为 ${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}。`
    : "当前还没有连接钱包，我只能先给出通用建议。";

  let reply = `${walletLine}\n\n`;

  if (/余额|balance|多少钱|资产/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "余额读取来自浏览器注入的钱包 provider，本地 Agent 只做解释，不持有私钥，也不会发起签名。";
  } else if (/风险|安全|risk|safe|授权/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "建议先检查当前网络、目标合约地址、授权额度和交易来源。任何转账或授权都应该留在 MetaMask 弹窗里由你确认。";
  } else if (/下一步|建议|next|操作/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "下一步可以先刷新余额，再让 Agent 汇总当前链、余额和目标操作。如果要做交易，先生成只读计划，不直接签名。";
  } else {
    reply += `我按 Codex 交互模式收到你的问题：“${message || "空消息"}”。你可以继续让我分析钱包余额、解释链 ID、做风险检查，或整理下一步操作。`;
  }

  return {
    reply,
    source: "mock-bridge",
    safety: {
      canReadWalletBalance: true,
      canSignTransactions: false,
      requiresUserWalletConfirmation: true,
    },
  };
}

function shortAddress(address) {
  return `${String(address).slice(0, 6)}...${String(address).slice(-4)}`;
}

function bindWalletEvents() {
  const provider = getProvider();
  if (!provider?.on) return;

  provider.on("accountsChanged", async (accounts) => {
    state.account = accounts[0] || "";
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });

  provider.on("chainChanged", async (chainId) => {
    state.chainId = chainId;
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });
}

els.connectWalletBtn.addEventListener("click", connectWallet);
els.refreshBalanceBtn.addEventListener("click", refreshBalance);
els.connectAgentBtn.addEventListener("click", connectAgent);
els.toggleSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleSettings);
els.chatForm.addEventListener("submit", sendChat);
els.clearChatBtn.addEventListener("click", clearChat);

bindWalletEvents();
renderWallet();
renderAgent();
renderMessages();

+function runProcess(command, args, timeoutMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const child = spawn(command, args, {
+      cwd: process.cwd(),
+      env: {
+        ...process.env,
+        NO_COLOR: "1",
+      },
+      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
+    });
+    let stdout = "";
+    let stderr = "";
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      child.kill("SIGTERM");
+      reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 超时：${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)} 秒未返回。`));
+    }, timeoutMs);
+
+    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stdout += chunk;
+    });
+    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderr += chunk;
+    });
+    child.on("error", (error) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      reject(error);
+    });
+    child.on("close", (exitCode) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      if (exitCode === 0) {
+        resolve({ exitCode, stdout, stderr });
+      } else {
+        reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 退出码 ${exitCode}。\n${stderr || stdout}`));
+      }
+    });
+  });
+}

我已经改成两种模式：

- 默认：`mock`，本地模拟回复。
- 真实 Codex：`codex-cli`，bridge 会执行本机 `codex exec`，拿 Codex 的最后回复返回页面。

真实 Codex 启动方式：

```bash
cd /Users/vincet/Documents/New\ project/agentwallet2
AGENT_WALLET_MODE=codex-cli CODEX_BIN=/Users/vincet/.openclaw/tools/node-v22.22.0/bin/codex node local-agent-bridge.mjs
```

我这边在沙箱里测试 `codex exec` 时遇到权限/网络/状态库错误，所以不能在当前受限环境里确认真实 Codex 调用成功；但代码已经接到 `codex exec` 入口了。你需要在本机普通终端启动上面的 `codex-cli` 模式。

import http from "node:http";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";

const HOST = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const PORT = Number(process.env.AGENT_WALLET_PORT || 18920);
const MODE = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_MODE || "mock";
const CODEX_BIN = process.env.CODEX_BIN || "codex";
const CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS || 120_000);

const profile = {
  id: "local-codex",
  name: MODE === "codex-cli" ? "Codex CLI Agent" : "Local Mock Agent",
  mode: MODE,
  status: "online",
  skills: ["wallet-summary", "balance-explain", "risk-check", "next-action", "codex-chat"],
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCors(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      sendJson(response, await routeChat(payload));
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat-stream") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeChat(payload);
      await sendStream(response, result.reply);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

server.l<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1104" />都必须由用户在 MetaMask 中确认。",

function buildMockReply(payload) {
  const message = String(payload.message || "").trim();
  const wallet = payload.wallet || {};
  const hasWallet = Boolean(wallet.address);
  const walletLine = hasWallet
    ? `当前钱包 ${shortAddress(wallet.address)} 在 ${wallet.chainId || "未知链"} 上余额为 ${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}。`
    : "当前还没有连接钱包，我只能先给出通用建议。";

  let reply = `${walletLine}\n\n`;

  if (/余额|balance|多少钱|资产/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "余额读取来自浏览器注入的钱包 provider，本地 Agent 只做解释，不持有私钥，也不会发起签名。";
  } else if (/风险|安全|risk|safe|授权/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "建议先检查当前网络、目标合约地址、授权额度和交易来源。任何转账或授权都应该留在 MetaMask 弹窗里由你确认。";
  } else if (/下一步|建议|next|操作/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "下一步可以先刷新余额，再让 Agent 汇总当前链、余额和目标操作。如果要做交易，先生成只读计划，不直接签名。";
  } else {
    reply += `我按 Codex 交互模式收到你的问题：“${message || "空消息"}”。你可以继续让我分析钱包余额、解释链 ID、做风险检查，或整理下一步操作。`;
  }

  return {
    reply,
    source: "mock-bridge",
    safety: {
      canReadWalletBalance: true,
      canSignTransactions: false,
      requiresUserWalletConfirmation: true,
    },
  };
}

function shortAddress(address) {
  return `${String(address).slice(0, 6)}...${String(address).slice(-4)}`;
}

function bindWalletEvents() {
  const provider = getProvider();
  if (!provider?.on) return;

  provider.on("accountsChanged", async (accounts) => {
    state.account = accounts[0] || "";
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });

  provider.on("chainChanged", async (chainId) => {
    state.chainId = chainId;
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });
}

els.refreshBalanceBtn.addEventListener("click", refreshBalance);
els.connectAgentBtn.addEventListener("click", connectAgent);
els.toggleSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleSettings);
els.chatForm.addEventListener("submit", sendChat);
els.clearChatBtn.addEventListener("click", clearChat);

bindWalletEvents();
renderWallet();
renderAgent();
renderMessages();

+function runProcess(command, args, timeoutMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const child = spawn(command, args, {
+      cwd: process.cwd(),
+      env: {
+        ...process.env,
+        NO_COLOR: "1",
+      },
+      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
+    });
+    let stdout = "";
+    let stderr = "";
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      child.kill("SIGTERM");
+      reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 超时：${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)} 秒未返回。`));
+    }, timeoutMs);
+
+    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stdout += chunk;
+    });
+    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderr += chunk;
+    });
+    child.on("error", (error) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      reject(error);
+    });
+    child.on("close", (exitCode) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      if (exitCode === 0) {
+        resolve({ exitCode, stdout, stderr });
+      } else {
+        reject(new Error(`Codex CLI 退出码 ${exitCode}。\n${stderr || stdout}`));
+      }
+    });
+  });
+}

import http from "node:http";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";

const HOST = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_HOST || "127.0.0.1";
const PORT = Number(process.env.AGENT_WALLET_PORT || 18920);
const MODE = process.env.AGENT_WALLET_MODE || "mock";
const CODEX_BIN = process.env.CODEX_BIN || "codex";
const CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.CODEX_TIMEOUT_MS || 120_000);

const profile = {
  id: "local-codex",
  name: MODE === "codex-cli" ? "Codex CLI Agent" : "Local Mock Agent",
  mode: MODE,
  status: "online",
  skills: ["wallet-summary", "balance-explain", "risk-check", "next-action", "codex-chat"],
};

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  setCors(response);

  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    response.writeHead(204);
    response.end();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url || "/", `http://${request.headers.host}`);

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, { ok: true, status: "online", mode: MODE });
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/profile") {
      sendJson(response, profile);
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      sendJson(response, await routeChat(payload));
      return;
    }

    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/chat-stream") {
      const payload = await readJson(request);
      const result = await routeChat(payload);
      await sendStream(response, result.reply);
      return;
    }

    sendJson(response, { error: "Not found" }, 404);
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, { error: error.message || "Bridge error" }, 500);
  }
});

server.l<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1104" />都必须由用户在 MetaMask 中确认。",
    "",
    "钱包上下文：",
    `- 地址：${wallet.address || "未连接"}`,
    `- Chain ID：${wallet.chainId || "未知"}`,
    `- 余额：${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}`,
    "",
    `用户问题：${message || "空消息"}`,
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildMockReply(payload) {
  const message = String(payload.message || "").trim();
  const wallet = payload.wallet || {};
  const hasWallet = Boolean(wallet.address);
  const walletLine = hasWallet
    ? `当前钱包 ${shortAddress(wallet.address)} 在 ${wallet.chainId || "未知链"} 上余额为 ${wallet.balance || "0.0000"} ${wallet.unit || "ETH"}。`
    : "当前还没有连接钱包，我只能先给出通用建议。";

  let reply = `${walletLine}\n\n`;

  if (/余额|balance|多少钱|资产/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "余额读取来自浏览器注入的钱包 provider，本地 Agent 只做解释，不持有私钥，也不会发起签名。";
  } else if (/风险|安全|risk|safe|授权/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "建议先检查当前网络、目标合约地址、授权额度和交易来源。任何转账或授权都应该留在 MetaMask 弹窗里由你确认。";
  } else if (/下一步|建议|next|操作/i.test(message)) {
    reply += "下一步可以先刷新余额，再让 Agent 汇总当前链、余额和目标操作。如果要做交易，先生成只读计划，不直接签名。";
  } else {
    reply += `我按 Codex 交互模式收到你的问题：“${message || "空消息"}”。你可以继续让我分析钱包余额、解释链 ID、做风险检查，或整理下一步操作。`;
  }

  return {
    reply,
    source: "mock-bridge",
    safety: {
      canReadWalletBalance: true,
      canSignTransactions: false,
      requiresUserWalletConfirmation: true,
    },
  };
}

function shortAddress(address) {
  return `${String(address).slice(0, 6)}...${String(address).slice(-4)}`;
}

function bindWalletEvents() {
  const provider = getProvider();
  if (!provider?.on) return;

  provider.on("accountsChanged", async (accounts) => {
    state.account = accounts[0] || "";
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });

  provider.on("chainChanged", async (chainId) => {
    state.chainId = chainId;
    if (state.account) await refreshBalance();
    renderWallet();
  });
}

els.connectWalletBtn.addEventListener("click", connectWallet);
els.refreshBalanceBtn.addEventListener("click", refreshBalance);
els.connectAgentBtn.addEventListener("click", connectAgent);
els.toggleSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleSettings);
els.chatForm.addEventListener("submit", sendChat);
els.clearChatBtn.addEventListener("click", clearChat);

bindWalletEvents();
renderWallet();
renderAgent();
renderMessages();
